[page 001]
                 Suttantapiṭake khuddakanikāyassa jātakaṃ
                         ---------
              namo tassa bhagavato arahato sammāsambuddhassa.
                       Ekanipātajātakaṃ 1-
                        1 apaṇṇakavaggo
                        1 apaṇṇakajātakaṃ
        [1] < PTS. J I , 104 >Apaṇṇakaṃ ṭhānameke dutiyaṃ āhu takkikā
            etadaññāya medhāvī taṃ gaṇhe yadapaṇṇakanti 2-.
                       Apaṇṇakajātakaṃ paṭhamaṃ.
                         ---------
                      2 Vaṇṇupathajātakaṃ 3-
            [2] < PTS. J I , 109 >Akilāsuno vaṇṇupathe 4- khaṇantā
                udaṅgaṇe tattha papaṃ avinduṃ
                evaṃ muni vīriyabalūpapanno 5-
                akilāsu vinde hadayassa santinti.
                      Vaṇṇupathajātakaṃ dutiyaṃ.

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote:1 Ma. ekakanipāta. 2 Po. taṃ gaṇheyya apaṇṇakaṃ. 3 Po. vaṇṇapathajātakaṃ.
 4 Po. vaṇṇapathe. 5. Ma. munī viriyabalūpapanno.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 002]
                       3 Serivavāṇijajātakaṃ
        [3] < PTS. J I , 113 >Idha ce 1- naṃ virādhesi saddhammassa niyāmakaṃ 2-
            ciraṃ tvaṃ anutappesi 3- serivāyaṃva vāṇijoti.
                      Serivavāṇijajātakaṃ tatiyaṃ.
                         ----------
                       4 Cullakaseṭṭhijātakaṃ
        [4] < PTS. J I , 122 >Appakenapi medhāvī pābhaṭena vicakkhaṇo
            samuṭṭhāpeti attānaṃ aṇuṃ aggiṃva sandhamanti.
                     Cullakaseṭṭhijātakaṃ catutthaṃ.
                         ----------
                      5 Taṇḍulanāḷijātakaṃ 4-
                [5] Kimagghatī taṇḍulanāḷikā ca
                    bārāṇasīantarabāhirāni
            assapañcasatetāni ekā taṇḍulanāḷikāti.
                      Taṇḍulanāḷijātakaṃ pañcamaṃ.
                           -------

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote:1 Sī. Yu. ce hi naṃ. 2 Ma. niyāmataṃ. 3. Sī. Yu. anutapessasi anutappissasi.
 4 Sī. idaṃ jātakaṃ na dissati. Ma. evaṃ dissati
  kimagghati taṇḍulanāḷikāyaṃ assāna mūlāya vadehi rāja
  bārāṇasīsantarabāhirato ayamagghati taṇḍulanāḷikā.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 003]
                        6 Devadhammajātakaṃ
        [6] < PTS. J I , 129 >Hiriottappasampannā sukkadhammasamāhitā
            santo sappurisā loke devadhammāti vuccareti.
                       Devadhammajātakaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ.
                           -------
                       7 Kaṭṭhahārijātakaṃ 1-
        [7] < PTS. J I , 135 >Putto tyāhaṃ mahārāja tvaṃ maṃ posa janādhipa
            aññepi devo poseti kiñca devo sakaṃ pajanti.
                       Kaṭṭhahārijātakaṃ sattamaṃ.
                          --------
                         8 Gāmanijātakaṃ
        [8] < PTS. J I , 136 >Api ataramānānaṃ phalāsāva samijjhati
            vipakkabrahmacariyosmi evaṃ jānāhi gāmanīti.
                        Gāmanijātakaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ.
                          --------
                       9 Maghadevajātakaṃ 2-
        [9] < PTS. J I , 138 >Uttamaṅgaruhā mayhaṃ ime jātā vayoharā
            pātubhūtā devadūtā pabbajjāsamayo mamāti.
                       Maghadevajātakaṃ navamaṃ.

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote:1 Po. kaṭṭhavāhanajātakaṃ. 2 Po. devadūtajātakaṃ.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 004]
                        10 Sukhavihārijātakaṃ
       [10] < PTS. J I , 141 >Yañca aññe na rakkhanti yo ca aññe na rakkhati
            save rāja sukhaṃ seti kāmesu anapekkhavāti.
                       Sukhavihārijātakaṃ dasamaṃ.
                       Apaṇṇakavaggo paṭhamo.
                          --------
                           Tassuddānaṃ
                varāpaṇṇaka vaṇṇapatha 1- serivaro
                suvicakkhaṇa taṇḍulanāḷikassā
                hiri putta varuttagāmaninā
                yo ca na rakkhati tena dasa 2-.
                          --------
                          2 Sīlavaggo
                        1 lakkhaṇajātakaṃ 3-
       [11] < PTS. J I , 144 >Hoti sīlavataṃ attho paṭisanthāravuttinaṃ
            lakkhaṇaṃ passa āyantaṃ ñātisaṅghapurakkhitaṃ 4-
            atha passasimaṃ kāḷaṃ suvihīnaṃva ñātibhīti.
                         Lakkhaṇajātakaṃ paṭhamaṃ
                           -------

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote:1 Ma. vaṇṇupatha. 2 Ma. sabbattha dasāti. 3 Ma. lakkhaṇamigajātakaṃ.
 4 Ma. ñātisaṅghapurakkhataṃ.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 005]
                        2 Nigrodhamigajātakaṃ
       [12] < PTS. J I , 152 >Nigrodhameva seveyya na sākhaṃ upasaṃvase
            nigrodhasmiṃ mataṃ seyyo yañce sākhasmi jīvitanti.
                         Nigrodhajātakaṃ dutiyaṃ.
                            --------
                           3 Kaṇḍinajātakaṃ
       [13] < PTS. J I , 155 >Dhiratthu kaṇḍinaṃ sallaṃ purisaṃ gāḷhavedhinaṃ
            dhiratthu taṃ janapadaṃ yatthitthī pariṇāyikā
            te vāpi 1- dhikkitā sattā ye itthīnaṃ vasaṃ gatāti.
                          Kaṇḍinajātakaṃ tatiyaṃ.
                            --------
                           4 Vātamigajātakaṃ
           [14] < PTS. J I , 158 >Na kiratthi rasehi pāpiyo
                āvāsehi vā 2- santhavehi vā
                vātamigaṃ gahananissitaṃ
                vasamānesi rasehi sañjayoti.
                        Vātamigajātakaṃ catutthaṃ.
                           --------
                         5 Kharādiyajātakaṃ
       [15] < PTS. J I , 160 >Aṭṭhakkhuraṃ kharādiye migaṃ vaṅkātivaṅkinaṃ

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote:1 Ma. cāpi. 2 Ma. va.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 006]
            Sattakālehatikkantaṃ 1- na naṃ ovaditussaheti.
                      Kharādiyajātakaṃ pañcamaṃ.
                          --------
                       6 Tipallatthamigajātakaṃ
           [16] < PTS. J I , 163 >Migaṃ tipallatthamanekamāyaṃ
                aṭṭhakkhuraṃ aḍḍharattāpapāyiṃ 2-
                ekena sotena chamāssasanto
                chahi kalāhatibhoti 3- bhāgineyyoti.
                      Tipallatthamigajātakaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ.
                          --------
                         7 Mālutajātakaṃ
       [17] < PTS. J I , 165 >Kāḷe vā yadi vā juṇhe yadā vāyati māluto
            vātajāni hi sītāni ubhotthamaparājitāti.
                       Mālutajātakaṃ sattamaṃ.
                           -------
                        8 Matakabhattajātakaṃ
       [18] < PTS. J I , 168 >Evañce sattā jāneyyuṃ dukkhāyaṃ jātisambhavo
            na pāṇo pāṇinaṃ haññe pāṇaghātī hi socatīti.
                      Matakabhattajātakaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ.

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote:1 Sī. Ma. sattahi kālātikkantaṃ. 2. Sī. Yu. aḍḍharattāvapāyiṃ.
 3 Ma. kalāhitibhoti.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 007]
                       9 Āyācitabhattajātakaṃ
       [19] < PTS. J I , 169 >Sace muñce 1- pecca muñce 2- muccamāno hi bajjhasi 3-
            na hevaṃ dhīrā muccanti mutti bālassa bandhananti.
                      Āyācitabhattajātakaṃ navamaṃ.
                          ---------
                        10 Naḷapānajātakaṃ
       [20] < PTS. J I , 171 >Disvā padamanuttiṇṇaṃ disvānotaritaṃ padaṃ
            naḷena vāriṃ pivissāma 4- neva maṃ tvaṃ vadhissasīti.
                       Naḷapānajātakaṃ dasamaṃ.
                        Sīlavaggo dutiyo.
                           -------
                           Tassuddānaṃ
                atha lakkhaṇa sākha dhiratthu
                puna nakiratthi rasehi kharādiyā
                atibhoti rasamāluta 5- pāṇa
                muccena naḷāvhayanena bhavanti dasa.
                          --------
                         3 Kuruṅgavaggo
                        1 kuruṅgamigajātakaṃ
       [21] < PTS. J I , 174 >Ñātametaṃ kuruṅgassa yaṃ tvaṃ sepaṇṇi seyyasi 6-

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote:1-2 Ma. mucce. ito paraṃ īdisameva. 3 Ma. bajjhati. 4 Ma. pissāma.
 5 Ma. varamāluta. 6 Ma. siyyasi.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 008]
            Aññaṃ sepaṇṇiṃ gacchāmi na me te ruccate phalanti.
                       Kuruṅgamigajātakaṃ paṭhamaṃ.
                           --------
                          2 Kukkurajātakaṃ
            [22] < PTS. J I , 177 >Ye kukkurā rājakulasmi vaḍḍhā
                 koleyyakā vaṇṇabalūpapannā
                 teme na vajjhā mayamasma vajjhā
                 nāyaṃ saghaccā dubbalaghātikāyanti.
                         Kukkurajātakaṃ dutiyaṃ.
                           --------
                       3 Bhojājānīyajātakaṃ 1-
       [23] < PTS. J I , 180 >Api passena semāno sallebhi sallalīkato
            seyyova vaḷavā bhojjho 2- yuñja maññeva sārathīti.
                        Bhojājānīyajātakaṃ tatiyaṃ.
                           ---------
                          4 Ājaññajātakaṃ
       [24] < PTS. J I , 181 >Yadā yadā yattha yadā yattha yattha yadā yadā
            ājañño kurute vegaṃ hāyanti tattha vāḷavāti.
                        Ājaññajātakaṃ catutthaṃ.
                           ---------

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote:1 Ma. gojānīyajātakaṃ. 2 Ma. gojo.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 009]
                          5 Titthajātakaṃ
       [25] < PTS. J I , 185 >Aññamaññehi titthehi assaṃ pāyehi sārathi
            accāsanassa puriso pāyāsassapi tappatīti.
                        Titthajātakaṃ pañcamaṃ.
                          --------
                        6 Mahiḷāmukhajātakaṃ
           [26] < PTS. J I , 188 >Purāṇacorāna vaco nisamma
                mahiḷāmukho pothayamānucāri 1-
                susaññatānaṃ hi vaco nisamma
                gajuttamo sabbaguṇesu aṭṭhāti.
                        Mahiḷāmukhajātakaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ.
                          --------
                         7 Abhiṇhajātakaṃ
           [27] < PTS. J I , 190 >Nālaṃ kabalaṃ padātave
                na 2- piṇḍaṃ na kuse na ghaṃsituṃ
                maññāmi abhiṇhadassanā
                nāgo sinehamakāsi 3- kukkureti.
                       Abhiṇhajātakaṃ sattamaṃ.
                          --------

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote:1 Ma. pothayamanvacārī. 2 Ma. na ca piṇḍaṃ. 3 Ma. snehamakāsi.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 010]
                        8 Nandivisālajātakaṃ
       [28] < PTS. J I , 193 >Manuññameva bhāseyya nāmanuññaṃ kudācanaṃ
            manuññaṃ bhāsamānassa garubhāraṃ udaddhari
            dhanañca naṃ alābhesi tena cattamano ahūti.
                        Nandivisālajātakaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ.
                           ---------
                           9 Kaṇhajātakaṃ
        [29] < PTS. J I , 196 >Yato yato garu dhuraṃ yato gambhīravattani 1-
             tadāssu kaṇhaṃ yuñjanti savāssu taṃ vahate dhuranti.
                         Kaṇhajātakaṃ navamaṃ.
                           ---------
                          10 Muṇikajātakaṃ
       [30] < PTS. J I , 197 >Mā muṇikassa pihayi āturannāni bhuñjati
            appossukko bhusaṅkhāda etaṃ dīghāyulakkhaṇanti.
                          Muṇikajātakaṃ dasamaṃ.
                         Kuruṅgavaggo tatiyo.
                           ---------
                            Tassuddānaṃ
                kuruṅgassa kukkura bhojavaro 2-
                puna vāḷavassasirivayano 3-
                mahiḷāmukha nāmanuññavaro
                vahate dhura muṇikena dasa.

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote:1 Ma. gambhīravattanī. 2 Ma. gojavaro. 3 Ma. ... sirivahayano.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 011]
                         4 Kulāvakavaggo
                         1 kulāvakajātakaṃ
           [31] < PTS. J I , 203 >Kulāvakā mātali simbalismiṃ
                īsāmukhena parivajjayassu
                kāmaṃ cajāma asuresu pāṇaṃ
                māyime 1- dijā vikulāvā ahesunti.
                         Kulāvakajātakaṃ paṭhamaṃ.
                           -------
                          2 Naccajātakaṃ
           [32] < PTS. J I , 207 >Rudaṃ manuññaṃ rucirā ca piṭṭhi
                veḷuriyavaṇṇūpaṭibhā 2- ca gīvā
                byāmamattāni ca pekkhuṇāni
                naccena te dhītaraṃ no dadāmīti.
                         Naccajātakaṃ dutiyaṃ.
                            -------
                        3 Sammodamānajātakaṃ
       [33] < PTS. J I , 209 >Sammodamānā gacchanti jālamādāya pakkhino
            yadā te vivadissanti tadā ehinti me vasanti.
                       Sammodamānajātakaṃ tatiyaṃ.

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote:1 Ma. māme. 2 Ma. veḷuriyavaṇṇūpanibhā.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 012]
                          4 Macchajātakaṃ
       [34] < PTS. J I , 211 >Na maṃ sītaṃ na maṃ uṇhaṃ na maṃ jālasmi bādhanaṃ
            yañca maṃ maññate macchī aññaṃ so ratiyā gatoti.
                         Macchajātakaṃ catutthaṃ.
                            -------
                          5 Vaṭṭakajātakaṃ
       [35] < PTS. J I , 214 >Santi pakkhā apatanā santi pādā avañcanā

                        Vaṭṭakajātakaṃ pañcamaṃ.
                            -------
                          6 Sakuṇajātakaṃ
       [36] < PTS. J I , 216 >Yannissitā jagatiruhaṃ (vihaṅgamā) svāyaṃ aggiṃ pamuñcati
            disā bhajatha vaṅkaṅgā 1- jātaṃ saraṇato bhayanti.
                          Sakuṇajātakaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ.
                            -------
                          7 Tittirajātakaṃ
       [37] < PTS. J I , 219 >Ye vuḍḍhamapacāyanti narā dhammassa kovidā
            diṭṭhe dhamme ca pāsaṃsā samparāyo ca suggatīti 2-.
                         Tittirajātakaṃ sattamaṃ.

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote:1 Ma. vakkaṅgā. 2 Ma. diṭṭheva dhamme pāsaṃsā samparāye ca suggatiṃ.

                            8 Bakajātakaṃ
        [38] < PTS. J I , 223 >Nāccantaṃ nikatippañño nikatyā sukhamedhati

       [39] < PTS. J I , 226 >Maññe sovaṇṇayo rāsi sovaṇṇamālā ca nandako

       [40] < PTS. J I , 233 >Kāmaṃ patāmi nirayaṃ uddhapādo 2- avaṃsiro

                            Tassuddānaṃ
                siri mātali dhītara pakkhivaro
                ratirāgato 3- mātāpitā ca puna

                tathā nandaka piṇḍavarena dasa.

                         5 Pañcāvudhajātakaṃ
      [55] < PTS. J I , 275 >Yo alīnena cittena alīnamanaso naro
           bhāveti kusalaṃ dhammaṃ yogakkhemassa pattiyā

                        6 Kañcanakkhandhajātakaṃ

                         2 Santhavajātakaṃ
          [173] < PTS. J II , 44 >Na santhavasmā paramatthi pāpiyo
                yo santhavo kāpurisena hoti
                santappito sappinā pāyāsena
                kicchā kataṃ paṇṇakuṭiṃ adaḍḍhahi 1-.
          [174] < PTS. J II , 44 >Na santhavasmā paramatthi seyyo
                yo santhavo sappurisena hoti
                sīhassa bayagghassa ca dīpino ca
                sāmā mukhaṃ lehati santhavenāti.
                        Santhavajātakaṃ dutiyaṃ.

                          3 Susīmajātakaṃ
          [175] < PTS. J II , 48 >Kāḷā migā setadantā tavīme

                anussaraṃ pettipitāmahānaṃ.
          [176] < PTS. J II , 49 >Kāḷā migā setadantā mamīme
                parosataṃ hemajālābhichannā
                te te dadāmīti vadāmi māṇava

                         Sādhusīlajātakaṃ dasamaṃ
                         ruhakavaggo pañcamo.

                cirāagata koṭṭha sarīra dasa.

      [256] Appamāṇo buddho appamāṇo dhammo appamāṇo

                macchānugo soriva suṃsumāroti.
                       Vikaṇṇakajātakaṃ tatiyaṃ.

      [317] < PTS. J II , 230 >Tvamevadānimakari 1- yaṃ kāmo byagamā tayi
            soyaṃ appaṭisandhiko kharā chinnaṃva renukaṃ 2-.
      [318] < PTS. J II , 231 >Atricchā 3- atilobhena atilobhamadena ca
            evaṃ hāyati atthamhā ahaṃva asitābhuyāti.
                       Asitābhujātakaṃ catutthaṃ.

      [319] < PTS. J II , 232 >Sukhā gharā vacchanakha sahiraññā sabhojanā

      [320] < PTS. J II , 233 >Gharā nānīhamānassa gharā nābhaṇato musā
            gharā nādinnadaṇḍassa paresaṃ anikubbato
            evaṃ chiddaṃ durabhisambhavaṃ 5- ko gharaṃ paṭipajjatīti.
                        Vacchanakhajātakaṃ pañcamaṃ.

      [471] < PTS. J II , 430 >Ñātīnañca piyo hoti mittesu ca virocati

                      Abbhantaravaggo catuttho.

      [549] < PTS. J III , 38 >Eso hi uttaritaro bhārāvaho dhurandharo 5-

  Footnote:1 Ma. gahesuṃ. 2 Ma. yaṃ nu. 3 Ma. appenti. 4 Ma. sandhīyare.
 5 Ma. bhāravaho dhuraddharo.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 137]
            Yo paresādhipannānaṃ sayaṃ sandhātumarahatīti.
                        Kassapamandiyajātakaṃ dutiyaṃ.

                          3 Khantivādijātakaṃ
      [550] < PTS. J III , 42 >Yo te hatthe ca pāde ca kaṇṇanāsañca chedayi

      [551] < PTS. J III , 42 >Yo me hatthe ca pāde ca kaṇṇanāsañca chedayi
            ciraṃ jīvatu so rājā nahi kujjhanti mādisā.
      [552] < PTS. J III , 43 >Ahu atītamaddhāne 2- samaṇo khantidīpano
            taṃ khantiyāyeva ṭhitaṃ kāsirājā achedayi.
      [553] < PTS. J III , 43 >Tassa kammapharusassa 3- vipāko kaṭuko ahu
            yaṃ kāsirājā vedesi nirayamhi samappitoti.
                       Khantivādijātakaṃ tatiyaṃ.

      [556] < PTS. J III , 47 >Natthi anto kuto anto na anto paṭidissati

            Tadāhi pakataṃ pāpaṃ mama tuyhañca mārisa 1-.
      [557] < PTS. J III , 47 >Sohaṃ nūna ito gantvā yoniṃ laddhāna mānusiṃ
            vadaññū sīlasampanno kāhāmi kusalaṃ bahunti.
                       Lohakumbhijātakaṃ catutthaṃ.

      [558] < PTS. J III , 49 >Pharusā vata te vācā maṃsaṃ yācanako asi

                          8 Kaṇaverajātakaṃ

          [632] < PTS. J III , 108 >Caje cajantaṃ vanathaṃ na kayirā
                apetacittena na sambhajeyya
                dijo dumaṃ khīṇaphalaṃva 1- ñatvā
                aññaṃ samekkheyya mahā hi loko.
          [633] < PTS. J III , 109 >So te karissāmi yathānubhāvaṃ
                kataññutaṃ khattiyo 2- pekkhamā no
                sabbañca te issariyaṃ dadāmi
                yassicchasī tassa tuvaṃ dadāmīti.
                        Godhajātakaṃ tatiyaṃ.

                        4 Rājovādajātakaṃ

      [635] < PTS. J III , 111 >Evameva manussesu yo hoti seṭṭhasammato

      [637] < PTS. J III , 111 >Evameva manussesu yo hoti seṭṭhasammato
            so cepi 5- dhammaṃ carati pageva itarā pajā

[page 152]
            Sabbaṃ raṭṭhaṃ sukhaṃ seti rājā ce hoti dhammikoti.

                        3 Venasākhajātakaṃ 1-

                         10 Palāsajātakaṃ

            yathā pitā ca mātā ca evameso 2- bhavissati.
      [800] < PTS. J III , 209 >Yaṃ tvaṃ aṅkasmiṃ vaḍḍhesi khīrarukkhaṃ bhayānakaṃ
            āmanta kho taṃ gacchāma vuḍḍhimassa na ruccati.
      [801] < PTS. J III , 210 >Idāni kho maṃ bhāyati 3- mahānerunidassanaṃ
            haṃsassa anabhiññāya mahā me bhayamāgataṃ.
          [802] < PTS. J III , 210 >Na tassa vuḍḍhi kusalappasatthā
                yo vaḍḍhamāno ghasate patiṭṭhaṃ
                tassūparodhaṃ parisaṅkamāno
                patārayī mūlavadhāya dhīroti.
                       Palāsajātakaṃ dasamaṃ.
                    Vaṇṇārohavaggo dutiyo 4-.

                            Tassuddānaṃ
                atha vaṇṇa sasīla hiri labhate
                sumukhāsi sasāliya 4- mittavaro
                atha cakka palāsa sarāja sato
                yava bāla kapotaka paṇṇarasa 5-.
                           ---------
                Tatra vagguddānaṃ bhavati.
                Jinañca vaṇṇaṃ asamaṃ vaguppari
                sudesitā jātakanti santi vīsati
                mahesino brahmacarittamutta
                mavoca gāthā atthavatī subyañjanā.
                        Pañcakanipātaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ.

                atha passa sasūci ca tuṇḍilako
                miga mayhakapañcamapakkhivaro
                atha pañjali vārija majjha puna
                atha vaṭṭaka kapoṭavarena dasa.

                        9 Kapijātakaṃ
     [1028] < PTS. J III , 357 >Yattha verī nivisati 1- na vase tattha paṇḍito

            ubhinnamatthaṃ carati attano ca parassa ca.

                 amocayī gayhaka nīyamānaṃ

                 suttappabuddhova anussarāmi.
          [1040] < PTS. J III , 361 >Gaṅgāya sotasmiṃ gahītanāvaṃ

[page 223]

          [1041] < PTS. J III , 361 >Kappo ca te patthacaro ahosiṃ 1-

                        2 Mahākapijātakaṃ

                      11 Parantapajātakaṃ
     [1114] < PTS. J III , 418 >Āgamissati me pāpaṃ āgamissati me bhayaṃ
            tadā hi calitā sākhā manussena migena vā.
     [1115] < PTS. J III , 419 >Bhīruyā nūna me kāmo avidūre vasantiyā
            karissati kisaṃ paṇḍuṃ sāva sākhā parantapaṃ.
     [1116] < PTS. J III , 419 >Socayissati maṃ kantā gāme vasaṃ aninditā
            karissati kisaṃ paṇḍuṃ sāva sākhā parantapaṃ.
     [1117] < PTS. J III , 419 >Tayā maṃ hasitāpaṅgī 1- mihitāni 2- bhaṇitāni ca
            kisaṃ paṇḍuṃ karissati sāva sākhā parantapaṃ.
     [1118] < PTS. J III , 420 >Agamā nūna so saddo asaṃsi nūna so tava
            akkhātaṃ nūna taṃ tena yo taṃ sākhamakampayi.
     [1119] < PTS. J III , 420 >Idaṃ kho taṃ samāgamma mama bālassa cintitaṃ
            tadā hi calitā sākhā manussena migena vā.
     [1120] < PTS. J III , 420 >Tatheva tvaṃ avedasi avañci pitaraṃ mama
            hantvā sākhāhi chādento āgamissati te 3- bhayanti.
                   Parantapajātakaṃ ekādasamaṃ.
                    Gandhāravaggo dutiyo.

[page 237]
                         Tassuddānaṃ
                 varagāma mahākapi bhaggava ca
                 daḷhadhamma sakuñjara kesavaro
                 urago vidhūro puna jāgarataṃ
                 atha kosalādhipa parantapa ca.
                        --------
                     Tatra vagguddānaṃ bhavati.
             Atha sattanipātamhi vaggaṃ me bhaṇato suṇa
             kukku ca puna gandhāro dve ca vuttā mahesinā.
                     Sattakanipātaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ.
                       ----------

                      Aṭṭhakanipātajātakaṃ
                       1 kaccānivaggo
                       1 kaccānijātakaṃ
          [1121] < PTS. J III , 425 >Odātavatthā suci allakesā
                 kaccāni kiṃ kumbhimadhissayitvā 1-
                 piṭṭhā tilā dhovasi taṇḍulāni
                 tilodano hehiti kissa hetu.
          [1122] < PTS. J III , 425 >Na kho ayaṃ brāhmaṇa bhojanatthaṃ 2-
                 tilodano hehiti sādhupakko
                 dhammo mato tassa pahūnamajja 3-
                 ahaṃ karissāmi susānamajjhe.
          [1123] < PTS. J III , 426 >Anuvicca kaccāni karohi kiccaṃ
                 dhammo mato ko nu tavetasaṃsi 4-
                 sahassanetto atulānubhāvo
                 na miyyatī dhammavaro kadāci.
          [1124] < PTS. J III , 426 >Daḷhappamāṇaṃ mama ettha brahme
                 dhammo mato natthi mamettha kaṅkhā
                 ye yevadāni pāpā bhavanti

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote:1 Yu. kumbhimapassayitvā. 2 Sī. Ma. bhojanatthā. 3 Sī. pahūtamajja.
 Ma. pahuttamajja. Yu. bahūtamajja. 4 Ma. tveva saṃsi.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 239]
                 Te tevadāni sukhitā bhavanti.
                 Suṇisā hi mayhaṃ vajjhā ahosi
                 sā maṃ vadhitvāna vijāyi puttaṃ
                 sādāni sabbassa kulassa issarā
                 ahaṃ vasāmi 1- apaviṭṭhā ekikā.
          [1125] < PTS. J III , 426 >Jīvāmi vohaṃ na matohamasmi 2-
                 taveva atthāya idhāgatosmi
                 yā taṃ vadhitvāna vijāyi puttaṃ
                 sahāva puttena karomi bhasmaṃ.
          [1126] < PTS. J III , 427 >Evañca te ruccati devarāja
                 mameva atthāya idhāgatosi
                 ahañca putto suṇisā ca nattā
                 sammodamānā gharamāvasema.
          [1127] < PTS. J III , 427 >Evañca te ruccati kātiyāni
                 hatāpi santā na jahāsi dhammaṃ
                 tuvañca putto suṇisā ca nattā
                 sammodamānā gharamāvasetha.
          [1128] < PTS. J III , 427 >Sā kātiyānī suṇisāya saddhiṃ
                 sammodamānā gharamāvasittha

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote:1 Sī. Ma. panamhi. 2 Sī. Yu. nāhaṃ matosmi.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 240]
                 Putto ca nattā ca upaṭṭhahiṃsu.
                 Devānamindena adhiggahītāti.
                     Kaccānijātakaṃ paṭhamaṃ.

                      2 Aṭṭhasaddajātakaṃ
     [1129] < PTS. J III , 430 >Idaṃ pure ninnamāhu bahumacchaṃ mahodakaṃ
            āvāso bakarājassa pettikaṃ bhavanaṃ mama
            tyajja bhiṅgena 1- yāpema okaṃ na vijjahāmase 2-
     [1130] < PTS. J III , 430 >Ko dutiyaṃ asīlissa bandhurassakkhi bhejjati 3-
            ko me putte kulāvakaṃ mañca sotthiṃ karissati.
     [1131] < PTS. J III , 431 >Sabbā parikkhatā pheggu yāva tassā gatī ahu
            khīṇabhakkho mahārāja sāre na ramatī ghuṇo.
     [1132] < PTS. J III , 432 >Sā nūnāhaṃ ito gantvā rañño muttā nivesanā
            attānaṃ ramayissāmi dumasākhāniketinī 4-.
     [1133] < PTS. J III , 432 >So nūnāhaṃ ito gantvā rañño mutto nivesanā
            aggodakāni pivissāmi yūthassa purato vajaṃ.
     [1134] < PTS. J III , 432 >Taṃ maṃ kāmehi sammattaṃ rattaṃ kāmesu mucchitaṃ
            ānayī bharato luddho 5- bāhiko bhaddamatthu te.
     [1135] < PTS. J III , 433 >Andhakāratimisāyaṃ tuṅge upari pabbate

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote:1 Sī. Ma. bhekena. 2 Ma. vajahāmase. 3 Ma. bandharassakkhi bhecchati.
 4 Ma. dumasākhaniketinī. 5 Ma. sabbatla luddo.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 241]
            Sā maṃ saṇhena mudunā mā pādaṃ khaṇiyasmani 1-.
          [1136] < PTS. J III , 434 >Asaṃsayaṃ jātikhayantadassī
                 na gabbhaseyyaṃ punarāvaṭissaṃ 2-
                 ayamantimā pacchimagabbhaseyyā 3-
                 khīṇo me saṃsāro punabbhavāyāti.
                     Aṭṭhasaddajātakaṃ dutiyaṃ.

                       3 Sulasājātakaṃ
     [1137] < PTS. J III , 437 >Idaṃ suvaṇṇakāyūraṃ muttā veḷuriyā bahū
            sabbaṃ harassu bhaddante mañca dāsīti sāvaya.
     [1138] < PTS. J III , 437 >Oropayassu kalyāṇiṃ 4- mā bahuṃ 5- paridevasi
            na cāhaṃ abhijānāmi āgantvā 6- dhanamābhataṃ.
     [1139] < PTS. J III , 437 >Yato sarāmi attānaṃ yato pattāsmi viññutaṃ
            nevāhaṃ 7- abhijānāmi aññaṃ piyataraṃ tayā.
     [1140] < PTS. J III , 437 >Ehi taṃ upagūhissaṃ karissaṃ ca padakkhiṇaṃ
            na hidāni puna atthi mama tuyhañca saṅgamo.
     [1141] < PTS. J III , 438 >Na hi sabbesu ṭhānesu puriso hoti paṇḍito
            itthīpi paṇḍitā hoti tattha tattha vicakkhaṇā.
     [1142] < PTS. J III , 438 >Na hi sabbesu ṭhānesu puriso hoti paṇḍito

 7 Ma. na cāhaṃ.

            Itthīpi paṇḍitā hoti lahumatthaṃ vicintikā.
     [1143] < PTS. J III , 438 >Lahuñca vata khippañca nikaṭṭhe samacetayi
            migaṃ puṇṇāyataneva 1- sulasā sattukaṃ vadhi.
     [1144] < PTS. J III , 438 >Yodha uppatitaṃ atthaṃ na khippamanubujjhati

     [1145] < PTS. J III , 438 >Yodha 2- uppatitaṃ atthaṃ khippameva nibodhati

                       Sulasājātakaṃ tatiyaṃ
                       4 sumaṅgalajātakaṃ
          [1146] < PTS. J III , 441 >Bhusamhi kuddhoti apekkhiyāna
                 na tāva daṇḍaṃ panayeyya issaro
                 aṭṭhānaso appaṭirūpamattano
                 parassa dukkhāni bhusaṃ udīraye.
          [1147] < PTS. J III , 441 >Yato ca jāneyya pasādamattano
                 atthaṃ niyuñjeyya parassa dukkaṭaṃ
                 tadāyamatthoti sayaṃ avekkhiya
                 athassa daṇḍaṃ sadisaṃ nivesaye.
          [1148] < PTS. J III , 441 >Na cāpi jhāpeti paraṃ na attanaṃ
                 amucchito yo nayate nayānayaṃ

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote:1 Ma. puṇṇāyateneva. 2 Ma. yo ca.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 243]
                 Yo daṇḍadhāro bhavatīdha issaro
                 sa vaṇṇagutto siriyā na dhaṃsati.
          [1149] < PTS. J III , 441 >Ye khattiyāse anisammakārino
                 panenti daṇḍaṃ sahasā pamucchitā
                 avaṇṇasaṃyuttā 1- jahanti jīvitaṃ
                 ito vimuttāpica yanti duggatiṃ.
          [1150] < PTS. J III , 442 >Dhamme ca ye ariyappavedite ratā
                 anuttarā te vacasā manasā kammunā ca
                 te khantisoraccasamādhisaṇṭhitā 2-
                 vajanti lokaṃ dubhayaṃ tathāvidhā.
          [1151] < PTS. J III , 442 >Rājāhamasmi narapamudānamissaro
                 sacepi kujjhāmi ṭhapemi attanaṃ
                 nisedhayanto janataṃ tathāvidhaṃ
                 panemi daṇḍaṃ anukampa yoniso.
          [1152] < PTS. J III , 443 >Sirī ca lakkhī ca taveva khattiya
                 janādhipa mā vijahi kudācanaṃ
                 akkodhano niccapasannacitto
                 anigho tuvaṃ vassasatāni pālaya.
          [1153] < PTS. J III , 443 >Guṇehi etehi upehi 3- khattiya
                 ṭhitamariyavutti 4- suvaco akodhano

-------------------------------------------------
[page 244]
                 Sukhī anuppīḷa pasāsa medaniṃ
                 ito vimuttopi vajāhi suggatiṃ.
          [1154] < PTS. J III , 443 >Evaṃ sunītena 1- subhāsitena
                 dhammena ñāyena upāyaso nayaṃ
                 nibbāpaye saṅkhubhitaṃ mahājanaṃ
                 mahāva megho salilena medaninti 2-.

                       5 Gaṅgamālajātakaṃ
     [1155] < PTS. J III , 447 >Aṅgārajātā paṭhavī kukkulānugatā mahī
            atha gāyasi vattāni na taṃ tapati ātapo.
            Uddhaṃ tapati ādicco adho tapati vālukā
            atha gāyasi vattāni na taṃ tapati ātapo.
     [1156] < PTS. J III , 447 >Na maṃ tapati ātapo ātappā tapayanti maṃ
            atthā hi vividhā rāja te tapanti na ātapo.
     [1157] < PTS. J III , 450 >Addasaṃ kāma te mūlaṃ saṅkappā kāma jāyasi
            na taṃ saṅkappayissāmi evaṃ kāma na hehisi.
     [1158] < PTS. J III , 450 >Appāpi kāmā na alaṃ bahūhipi na tappati
            aho bālānalapanā 3- paṭivajjetha 4- jaggato.

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote:1 Yu. surinītena. 2 Ma. medininti. 3 Sī. Ma. ahahā bālalapanā.
 4 Ma. parivajjetha Sī. Yu. paṭivijjhetha.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 245]
          [1159] < PTS. J III , 450 >Appassa kammassa phalaṃ mamedaṃ
                 udayo ajjhagamā mahattapattaṃ
                 suladdhalābho vata māṇavassa
                 yo pabbaji kāmarāgaṃ pahāya.
          [1160] < PTS. J III , 452 >Tapasā pajahanti pāpakammaṃ
                 tapasā nhāpitakujjhakārabhāvaṃ 1-
                 tapasā abhibhuyya gaṅgamāla
                 nāmenālapasijja brahmadattaṃ.
          [1161] < PTS. J III , 453 >Sandiṭṭhikameva amma passatha
                 khantisoraccassa yo 2- vipāko
                 so 3- sabbajanassa vandito ahu
                 taṃ vandāma sarājikā samaccā.
          [1162] < PTS. J III , 453 >Mā kiñci avacuttha gaṅgamālaṃ
                 muninaṃ monapathesu sikkhamānaṃ
                 eso hi atari aṇṇavaṃ
                 yaṃ taritvā vicaranti 4- vītasokāti.
                     Gaṅgamālajātakaṃ pañcamaṃ.

                      6 Cetiyarājajātakaṃ
     [1163] < PTS. J III , 456 >Dhammo have hato hanti nāhato hanti kiñcinaṃ 1-
            tasmā hi dhammaṃ na hane mā taṃ 2- dhammo hato hani.
     [1164] < PTS. J III , 457 >Alikaṃ bhāsamānassa apakkamanti devatā
            pūtikañca mukhaṃ vāti sakaṭṭhānā ca dhaṃsati
            yo jānaṃ pucchito pañhaṃ aññathā naṃ viyākare
            sace hi saccaṃ bhaṇasi hohi rāja yathā pure
            musā ce bhāsase rāja bhūmiyaṃ tiṭṭha cetiya.
     [1165] < PTS. J III , 458 >Akāle vassatī tassa kāle tassa na vassati
            yo jānaṃ pucchito pañhaṃ aññathā naṃ viyākare
            sace hi saccaṃ bhaṇasi hohi rāja yathā pure
            musā ce bhāsase rāja bhūmiṃ pavisa cetiya.
     [1166] < PTS. J III , 458 >Jivhā tassa dvidhā hoti uragasseva disampati
            yo jānaṃ pucchito pañhaṃ aññathā naṃ viyākare
            sace hi saccaṃ bhaṇasi hohi rāja yathā pure
            musā ce bhāsase rāja bhiyyo pavisa cetiya.
     [1167] < PTS. J III , 459 >Jivhā tassa na bhavati macchasseva disampati
            yo jānaṃ pucchito pañhaṃ aññathā naṃ viyākare
            sace hi saccaṃ bhaṇasi hohi rāja yathā pure
            musā ce bhāsase rāja bhiyyo pavisa cetiya.

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote:1 Ma. kiñcanaṃ. 2 Ma. mā tvaṃ.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 247]
     [1168] < PTS. J III , 459 >Thiyova tassa jāyanti na pumā jāyare kule
            yo jānaṃ pucchito pañhaṃ aññathā naṃ viyākare
            sace hi saccaṃ bhaṇasi hohi rāja yathā pure
            musā ce bhāsase rāja bhiyyo pavisa cetiya.
     [1169] < PTS. J III , 459 >Puttā tassa na bhavanti pakkamanti disodisaṃ
            yo jānaṃ pucchito pañhaṃ aññathā naṃ viyākare
            sace hi saccaṃ bhaṇasi hohi rāja yathā pure
            musā ce bhāsase rāja bhiyyo pavisa cetiya.
     [1170] < PTS. J III , 460 >Sa rājā iminā patto 1- antalikkhacaro pure
            pāvekkhi paṭhaviṃ pecco hīnatto attapariyāyaṃ 2-
            tasmā hi chandāgamanaṃ nappasaṃsanti paṇḍitā
            aduṭṭhacitto bhāseyya giraṃ saccūpasañhitanti.
                     Cetiyarājajātakaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ.

     [1178] < PTS. J III , 468 >Sohaṃ sahassajīnova abandhu aparāyano

                        10 Dīpijātakaṃ
     [1199] < PTS. J III , 480 >Khamanīyaṃ yāpanīyaṃ kacci mātula te sukhaṃ
            sukhaṃ te ammā avaca sukhakāmā hi 3- te mayaṃ.
     [1200] < PTS. J III , 480 >Naṅguṭṭhaṃ me apakkamma 4- heṭhayitvāna eḷaki 5-

     [1201] < PTS. J III , 480 >Puratthābhimukho 6- nisinnosi ahante mukhamāgatā
            pacchato tuyhaṃ naṅguṭṭhaṃ kathaṃ khohaṃ avakkami.

[page 252]
     [1202] < PTS. J III , 481 >Yāvatā caturo dīpā sasamuddā sapabbatā
            tāvatā mayhaṃ naṅguṭṭhaṃ kathaṃ kho tvaṃ vivajjasi 1-.
     [1203] < PTS. J III , 481 >Pubbeva metaṃ akkhiṃsu mātā pitā ca bhātaro
            dīghaṃ duṭṭhassa naṅguṭṭhaṃ sāmhi vehāyasāgatā.
     [1204] < PTS. J III , 481 >Tañca disvāna āyantiṃ antalikkhasmi eḷaki 2-
            migasaṅgho palāyittha bhakkho me nāsito tayā.
     [1205] < PTS. J III , 481 >Iccevaṃ vilapantiyā eḷakiyā ruhagghaso
            galakaṃ anvāvamaddi natthi duṭṭhe subhāsitaṃ.
     [1206] < PTS. J III , 481 >Neva duṭṭhe nayo atthi na dhammo na subhāsitaṃ
            nikkamaṃ duṭṭhe yuñjetha so ca sabbhi na rajjatīti 3-.
                       Dīpijātakaṃ dasamaṃ.
                       Kaccānivaggo.

                         Tassuddānaṃ
                 parisuddhāmanāvilā vatthadharā
                 bakarājassa kāyuraṃ daṇḍavaro
                 atha aṅgāra cetiya devilinā
                 atha āditta gaṅgā daseḷakinā.
                     Aṭṭhakanipātaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ.

                       Navakanipātajātakaṃ
                        1 gijjhajātakaṃ
     [1207] < PTS. J III , 484 >Parisaṅkupatho nāma gijjhapatho 1- sanantano
            tatrāsi mātāpitaro gijjho posesi jiṇṇake.
     [1208] < PTS. J III , 484 >Tesaṃ ajagaraṃ medaṃ accahāsi bahutaso 2-
            pitā ca puttaṃ avaca jānaṃ uccaṃ papātinaṃ
            supattaṃ pakkhasampannaṃ 3- tejasiṃ dūragāminaṃ.
     [1209] < PTS. J III , 484 >Pariplavantaṃ paṭhaviṃ yadā tāta vijānahi
            sāgarena parikkhittaṃ cakkaṃva parimaṇḍalaṃ
            tato tāta nivattassu māssu etto paraṃ gami.
     [1210] < PTS. J III , 484 >Udapattosi 4- vegena balī pakkhī dijuttamo
            olokayanto vaṅkaṅgo pabbatāni vanāni ca.
     [1211] < PTS. J III , 484 >Addasa paṭhaviṃ gijjho yathāssāsi pitussutaṃ
            sāgarena parikkhittaṃ cakkaṃva parimaṇḍalaṃ.
     [1212] < PTS. J III , 484 >Tañca so samatikkamma parameva pavattatha 5-
            tañca vātasikhā tikkhā accahāsi baliṃ dijaṃ.
     [1213] < PTS. J III , 484 >Nāsakkhātigato poso punadeva nivattituṃ
            dijo byasanamāpādi verabbhānaṃ 6- vasaṃ gato.

 verambānaṃ.

                      2 Kosambiyajātakaṃ
     [1216] < PTS. J III , 488 >Puthusaddo samajano na bālo koci maññatha
            saṅghasmiṃ bhijjamānasmiṃ nāññaṃ bhiyyo amaññaruṃ.
     [1217] < PTS. J III , 488 >Parimuṭṭhā paṇḍitābhāsā vācāgocarabhāṇino
            yāvicchanti mukhāyāmaṃ yena nītā na taṃ vidū.
     [1218] < PTS. J III , 488 >Akkocchi maṃ avadhi maṃ ajini maṃ ahāsi me
            ye ca taṃ upanayhanti veraṃ tesaṃ na sammati.
     [1219] < PTS. J III , 488 >Akkocchi maṃ avadhi maṃ ajini maṃ ahāsi me
            ye ca taṃ nūpanayhanti veraṃ tesūpasammati.
     [1220] < PTS. J III , 488 >Na hi verena verāni sammantīdha kudācanaṃ
            averena ca sammanti esa dhammo sanantano.
     [1221] < PTS. J III , 488 >Pare ca na vijānanti mayamettha yamāmase
            ye ca tattha vijānanti tato sammanti medhagā.

                 kasmā tumhākaṃ no siyā.
          [1223] < PTS. J III , 488 >Sace labhetha nipakaṃ sahāyaṃ

     [1225] < PTS. J III , 488 >Ekassa caritaṃ seyyo natthi bāle sahāyatā

                       10 Samuggajātakaṃ
          [1292] < PTS. J III , 528 >Kuto nu āgacchatha bho tayo janā
                 svāgatā 2- etha nisīdathāsane
                 kaccittha bhonto kusalaṃ anāmayaṃ
                 cirassamabbhāgamanaṃ hi vo idha.
          [1293] < PTS. J III , 529 >Ahameva eko idhamajja patto
                 na cāpi me dutiyo koci vijjati
                 kimeva sandhāya te bhāsitaṃ ise
                 kuto nu āgacchatha bho tayo janā.
          [1294] < PTS. J III , 529 >Tvañca eko bhariyā ca te piyā

[page 266]
                 Samuggapakkhittanikiṇṇamantare

                 vāyussa puttena sahā tahiṃ ratā.
          [1295] < PTS. J III , 529 >Saṃviggarūpo isinā byākato 1-
                 so dānavo tattha samuggamuggili 2-

                       11 Pūtimaṃsajātakaṃ
     [1301] < PTS. J III , 534 >Na kho ruccati me āḷi pūtimaṃsassa pekkhanā

     [1304] < PTS. J III , 534 >Na akāle vipekkheyya kāle pekkheyya paṇḍito
            pūtimaṃsova pajjhāyi yo akāle vipekkhati.

          [1317] < PTS. J III , 541 >Bandhā kulikā 3- mitamāḷhakena

          [1318] < PTS. J III , 542 >Tānissa kammāyatanāni assu

                      1 catudvārajātakaṃ

     [1320] < PTS. J IV , 4 >Sabbe apihitā dvārā oruddhosmi yathā dijo

     [1321] < PTS. J IV , 4 >Laddhā satasahassāni atirekāni vīsati

            catubbhi aṭṭhajjhagamā aṭṭhābhipi ca soḷasa.
     [1323] < PTS. J IV , 4 >Soḷasābhi ca battiṃsa atricchaṃ cakkamāsado

     [1324] < PTS. J IV , 4 >Upari visālaṃ duppūraṃ icchāvisatagāminaṃ 3-

            yesaṃ cetaṃ asaṅkhātaṃ te honti cakkadhārino.
          [1326] < PTS. J IV , 5 >Kammaṃ samekkhe vipulañca bhogaṃ

[page 272]
                 Icchaṃ na seveyya anatthasañhitaṃ

     [1327] < PTS. J IV , 6 >Kīvacirannu me yakkha cakkaṃ sirasi ṭhassati

                       3 Catuposathajātakaṃ
          [1342] Yo kopaneyye na karoti kopaṃ
                 na kujjhati sappuriso kadāci
                 kuddhopi so nāvikaroti kopaṃ
                 taṃ ve naraṃ samaṇamāhu loke.
          [1343] Onodaro yo sahate jighacchaṃ
                 danto tapassī mitapānabhojano
                 āhārahetu na karoti pāpaṃ
                 taṃ ve naraṃ samaṇamāhu loke.
          [1344] Khiḍḍaṃ ratiṃ vippajahitvāna sabbaṃ
                 na cālikaṃ bhāsati kiñci loke
                 vibhūsanaṭṭhānā virato methunasmā
                 taṃ ve naraṃ samaṇamāhu loke.
          [1345] Pariggahaṃ lobhadhammañca sabbaṃ
                 yo ve pariññāya pariccajeti
                 dantaṃ ṭhitattaṃ amamaṃ nirāsaṃ
                 taṃ ve naraṃ samaṇamāhu loke.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 275]
          [1346] Pucchāma kattāramanomapañña 1-
                 kathāsu no viggaho atthi jāto
                 chindajja kaṅkhaṃ vicikicchitāni
                 tadajja kaṅkhaṃ vitaremu sabbe.
          [1347] Ye paṇḍitā atthadasā bhavanti
                 bhāsanti te yoniso tattha kāle
                 kathannu kathānaṃ abhāsitānaṃ
                 atthaṃ nayeyyuṃ kusalā janindā.
          [1348] Kathaṃ have bhāsati nāgarājā
                 garuḷo pana venateyyo kimāha
                 gandhabbarājā pana kiṃ vadesi
                 kathaṃ pana kurūnaṃ rājaseṭṭho.
          [1349] Khantiṃ have bhāsati nāgarājā
                 appāhāraṃ garuḷo venateyyo
                 gandhabbarājā rativippahānaṃ
                 akiñcanaṃ kurūnaṃ rājaseṭṭho.
          [1350] Sabbāni etāni subhāsitāni
                 na hettha dubbhāsitamatthi kiñci
                 yasmiñca etāni patiṭṭhitāni

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote:1 Ma. - paññaṃ.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 276]
                 Arāvanabbhā 1- susamohitāni
                 catubbhi dhammehi samaṅgibhūtaṃ
                 taṃ ve naraṃ samaṇamāhu loke.
          [1351] Tuvaṃ hi 2- seṭṭho tvamanuttarosi
                 tvaṃ dhammagū dhammavidū sumedho
                 paññāya pañhaṃ samaviggahetvā 3-
                 acchejji 4- dhīro vicikicchitāni
                 acchejji 4- kaṅkhaṃ vicikicchitāni
                 chinde 5- yathā nāgadantaṃ kharena.
          [1352] Nīluppalābhaṃ vimalaṃ anagghaṃ
                 vatthaṃ idaṃ dhūmasamānavaṇṇaṃ
                 pañhassa veyyākaraṇena tuṭṭho
                 dadāmi te dhammapūjāya dhīra.
          [1353] Suvaṇṇamālaṃ satapattaphullitaṃ
                 sakesaraṃ ratnasahassamaṇḍitaṃ
                 pañhassa veyyākaraṇena tuṭṭho
                 dadāmi te dhammapūjāya dhīra.
          [1354] Maṇiṃ anagghaṃ ruciraṃ pabhassaraṃ
                 kaṇṭhāvasattaṃ maṇibhūsitamme

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote:1 ārāvanābhyātipi arāpanabyetipi. Ma. arāvanābhyā. 2 Sī. Yu. tuvaṃ nu.
 3 Ma. samadhiggahetvā. 4 Ma. acchecchi. 5 Ma. cundo.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 277]
                 Pañhassa veyyākaraṇena tuṭṭho
                 dadāmi te dhammapūjāya dhīra.
          [1355] Gavaṃ sahassaṃ usabhañca nāgaṃ
                 ājaññayutte ca rathe dasa ime
                 pañhassa veyyākaraṇena tuṭṭho
                 dadāmi te gāmavarāni soḷasa.
     [1356] Sārīputto tadā nāgo supaṇṇo pana kolito
            gandhabbarājā anuruddho rājā ānandapaṇḍito
            vidhuro bodhisattova evaṃ dhāretha jātakanti.
                     Catuposathajātakaṃ tatiyaṃ.

                        4 Saṅkhajātakaṃ
          [1357] < PTS. J IV , 17 >Bahussuto sutadhammosi saṅkha

          [1362] < PTS. J IV , 19 >Idhannapānaṃ sayanāsanañca
                 yānāni nānāvividhāni saṅkha

          [1363] < PTS. J IV , 19 >Yaṅkiñci yiṭṭhañca hutañca mayhaṃ

                 sussoṇi subbhā suvilākamajjhe 2-
                 kissa me kammassa ayaṃ vipāko.

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote:1 Ma. sukhamāsisāno. 2 Ma. subbhamu suvilakkamajjhe.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 279]
          [1364] < PTS. J IV , 20 >Ghamme pathe brāhmaṇa ekabhikkhuṃ
                 ussaṭṭhapādaṃ 1- tasitaṃ kilantaṃ
                 paṭipādayi saṅkha upāhanāni 2-
                 sā dakkhiṇā kāmaduhā tavajja.
          [1365] < PTS. J IV , 20 >Sā hotu nāvā phalakūpapannā
                 anavassutā erakavātayuttā

          [1366] < PTS. J IV , 21 >Sā tuṭṭhacittā sumanā patītā

                 upānayī nagaraṃ sādhurammanti.
                      Saṅkhajātakaṃ catutthaṃ.

            Sārambhā jāyate kodho sopi teneva ḍayhati.
     [1378] < PTS. J IV , 26 >Aggīva tiṇakaṭṭhasmiṃ kodho yassa pavaḍḍhati
            nihīyati tassa yaso kāḷapakkheva candimā.
     [1379] < PTS. J IV , 26 >Anindo 1- dhūmaketūva kodho yassūpasammati
            āpūrati tassa yaso sukkapakkheva candimāti.
                    Cullabodhijātakaṃ pañcamaṃ.

                     6 Maṇḍabyajātakaṃ 2-
          [1380] < PTS. J IV , 31 >Sattāhamevāhaṃ pasannacitto
                 puññatthiko ācari 3- brahmacariyaṃ
                 athāparaṃ yaṃ caritaṃ mamayidaṃ 4-
                 vassāni paññāsa samādhikāni
                 akāmako vāpi ahaṃ carāmi
                 etena saccena suvatthi hotu
                 hataṃ visaṃ jīvatu yaññadatto.
          [1381] < PTS. J IV , 31 >Yasmā dānaṃ nābhinandiṃ kadāci
                 disvānahaṃ atithiṃ vāsakāle
                 na cāpi me appiyataṃ aveduṃ
                 bahussutā samaṇabrāhmaṇā ca
                 akāmako vāpi ahaṃ dadāmi

 Yu. mamāyidaṃ.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 282]
                 Etena saccena suvatthi hotu
                 hataṃ visaṃ jīvatu yaññadatto.
          [1382] < PTS. J IV , 32 >Āsīviso tāta pahūtatejo
                 yo taṃ aḍaṃsī biḷārā uddhacca 1-
                 tasmiñca me appiyatāya ajja
                 pitari ca te natthi koci viseso
                 etena saccena suvatthi hotu
                 hataṃ visaṃ jīvatu yaññadatto.
          [1383] < PTS. J IV , 33 >Santā dantā ye ca 2- paribbajanti
                 aññatra kaṇhā anakāmarūpā 3-
                 dīpāyana kissa jigucchamāno
                 akāmako carasi brahmacariyaṃ.
          [1384] < PTS. J IV , 33 >Saddhāya nikkhamma puna nivatto
                 so eḷamūgo capalo 4- vatāyaṃ
                 etassa vādassa jigucchamāno
                 akāmako carāmi brahmacariyaṃ
                 viññūpasatthañca satañca ṭhānaṃ
                 evampahaṃ puññakaro bhavāmi.
          [1385] < PTS. J IV , 34 >Samaṇe tuvaṃ brāhmaṇe addhike ca

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote:1 Ma. adaṃsī sacarā udicca. 2 Ma. dantāyeva. 3 Ma. natthākāmarūpā.
 4 Ma. eḷamūgova bālo.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 283]
                 Santappayāsi annapānena bhikkhaṃ
                 opānabhūtaṃva gharaṃ tavayidaṃ
                 annena pānena upetarūpaṃ
                 atha kissa vādassa jigucchamāno
                 akāmako dānamimaṃ dadāsi.
          [1386] < PTS. J IV , 34 >Pitaro ca me āsu 1- pitāmahā ca
                 saddhā ahu 2- dānapatī vadaññū
                 taṃ kūlavattaṃ 3- anuvattamāno
                 māhaṃ kule antimagandhino 4- ahuṃ
                 etassa vādassa jigucchamāno
                 akāmako dānamimaṃ dadāmi.
          [1387] < PTS. J IV , 35 >Dahariṃ kumāriṃ asamatthapaññaṃ

                 na cāpi me appiyataṃ avedi
                 aññatra kāmā paricārayantā 5-
                 atha kena vaṇṇena mayā hi 6- bhoti
                 saṃvāsadhammo ahu evarūpo.
          [1388] < PTS. J IV , 35 >Ārā dūre nayidha kadāci atthi
                 paramparā nāma kule imasmiṃ
                 taṃ kūlavattaṃ 3- anuvattamānā

                 Māhaṃ kule antimagandhinī ahuṃ
                 etassa vādassa jigucchamānā

          [1389] < PTS. J IV , 36 >Maṇḍabya bhāsissaṃ abhāsaneyyaṃ 2-

                 puttapemā nayidha paratthi kiñci
                 so no ayaṃ jīvati yaññadattoti.

                       7 Nigrodhajātakaṃ
     [1390] < PTS. J IV , 41 >Na vāhametaṃ 4- jānāmi kovāyaṃ kassa cāri vā 5-

     [1391] < PTS. J IV , 41 >Tato galavinītena purisā nīhariṃsu maṃ

     [1392] < PTS. J IV , 41 >Etādisaṃ dummatinā akataññena 7- dubbhinā

     [1393] < PTS. J IV , 42 >Na vāhametaṃ jānāmi napi me koci saṃsati

            tuyhamhā labbhitā iddhi ettha me natthi saṃsayo.
     [1395] < PTS. J IV , 42 >Yathāpi vījaṃ aggismiṃ ḍayhati na virūhati

                      13 Cakkavākajātakaṃ

[page 296]
            Na ca me tādiso vaṇṇo cakkavāka yathā tuvaṃ.
     [1458] < PTS. J IV , 71 >Sampassaṃ attani veraṃ hiṃsāya 1- mānusiṃ pajaṃ
            utrasto ghasasī bhīto tena vaṇṇo tavediso.
     [1459] < PTS. J IV , 71 >Sabbalokaviruddhosi dhaṅka pāpena kammunā
            laddho piṇḍo na pīṇeti tena vaṇṇo tavediso.
     [1460] < PTS. J IV , 71 >Ahampi samma bhuñjāmi ahiṃsaṃ sabbapāṇinaṃ
            appossukko nirāsaṅko asoko akutobhayo.
     [1461] < PTS. J IV , 71 >So karassu ānubhāvaṃ vītivattassu sīliyaṃ
            ahiṃsāya caraṃ 2- loke piyo hohisi mammiva.
     [1462] < PTS. J IV , 71 >Yo na hanti na ghāteti na jināti na jāpaye
            mettaṃ so sabbabhūtesu veraṃ tassa na kenacīti
                    cakkavākajātakaṃ terasamaṃ.

                      14 Bhūripañhājātakaṃ
          [1463] Saccaṃ kira tuvampi 3- bhūripañña
                 yā tādisī sirī dhiti mati ca
                 na tāyate bhāvavasūpanītaṃ
                 yo yavakaṃ bhuñjasi appasūpaṃ.
          [1464] Sukhaṃ dukkhena paripācayanto
                 kālākālaṃ vicinaṃ chandachanno

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote:1 Ma. hiṃsayaṃ. 2 Ma. cara. 3 Ma. tvaṃ api.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 297]
                 Atthassa dvārāni apāpuranto
                 tenāhaṃ tussāmi yavodanena.
          [1465] Kālañca ñatvā abhijihanāya
                 mantehi atthaṃ paripācayitvā
                 vijambhisaṃ sīhavijambhitāni
                 tāyiddhiyā dakkhasi maṃ punāpi.
          [1466] Sukhīpi heke na karonti pāpaṃ
                 avaṇṇasaṃsaggabhayā puneke
                 pahūsamāno vipulatthacintī
                 kiṃkāraṇā me na karosi dukkhaṃ.
          [1467] Na paṇḍitā attasukhassa hetu
                 pāpāni kammāni samācaranti
                 dukkhena phuṭṭhā khalitāpi santā
                 chandā ca dosā na jahanti dhammaṃ.
     [1468] Yenakenaci vaṇṇena mudunā dāruṇena vā
            uddhare dīnamattānaṃ pacchā dhammaṃ samācare.
     [1469] Yassa rukkhassa chāyāya nisīdeyya sayeyya vā
            na tassa sākhaṃ bhañjeyya mittadubbho hi pāpako.
          [1470] Yassāpi 1- dhammaṃ puriso 2- vijaññā
                 ye cassa kaṅkhaṃ vinayanti santo

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote:1 Sī. yassa hi. 2 Sī. manujo.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 298]
                 Taṃ hissa dīpañca parāyanañca
                 na tena mettiṃ jirayetha 1- pañño.
          [1471] Alaso gihī kāmabhogī na sādhu
                 asaññato pabbajito na sādhu
                 rājā na sādhu anisammakārī
                 yo paṇḍito kodhano taṃ na sādhu.
     [1472] Nisamma khattiyo kayirā nānisamma disampati
            nisammakārino rāja yaso kittī ca vaḍḍhatīti.
                    Bhūripañhājātakaṃ cuddasamaṃ.

                     15 Mahāmaṅgalajātakaṃ
          [1473] < PTS. J IV , 75 >Kiṃsu naro jappamadhicca kāle

          [1474] < PTS. J IV , 75 >Yassa devā pitaro ca sabbe
                 siriṃsapā 2- sabbabhūtāni cāpi
                 mettāya niccaṃ apacitāni honti
                 bhūtesu ve sotthānaṃ tadāhu.
          [1475] < PTS. J IV , 76 >Yo sabbalokassa nivātavutti

     [1485] < PTS. J IV , 85 >Kinnu ummattarūpova kevalaṃ dvārakaṃ imaṃ

            saṅkhasilāpavāḷamayaṃ kārayissāmi te sasaṃ.
            Santi aññepi sasakā araññe vanagocarā
            tepi te ānayissāmi kīdisaṃ sasamicchasi.
     [1487] < PTS. J IV , 85 >Na vāhamete 1- icchāmi ye sasā paṭhaviṃsitā

     [1488] < PTS. J IV , 86 >So nūna madhuraṃ ñāti jīvitaṃ vijahissasi
            apatthayaṃ yo patthayasi candato sasamicchasi.
     [1489] < PTS. J IV , 86 >Evañce kaṇha jānāsi yadaññamanusāsasi
            kasmā pure mataṃ puttaṃ ajjāpi anusocasi.
     [1490] < PTS. J IV , 86 >Yaṃ na labbhā manussena amanussena vā puna
            jāto me mā marī putto kuto labbhā alabbhiyaṃ.

            sakkā ānayituṃ kaṇha yaṃ petamanusocasi.
     [1491] < PTS. J IV , 87 >Yassa etādisā assu amaccā purisapaṇḍitā
            yathā nijjhāpaye ajja ghato purisapaṇḍito.

            vārinā viya osiñci sabbaṃ nibbāpaye daraṃ.

          [1526] < PTS. J IV , 106 >Ekā nisinnā suci saññatūru 1-

          [1531] < PTS. J IV , 108 >Nāriṃ naro nijjhapayaṃ dhanena

          [1532] < PTS. J IV , 108 >Āyu ca vaṇṇo ca 2- manussaloke
                 nihiyyati manujānaṃ sugatte
                 teneva vaṇṇena dhanaṃpi tuyhaṃ
                 nihiyyati jiṇṇatarāsi ajja.
            Evaṃ me pekkhamānassa rājaputti yasassini
            hāyate vata te 3- vaṇṇo ahorattānamaccaye
            imināva tvaṃ vayasā rājaputti sumedhase

          [1533] < PTS. J IV , 109 >Devā na jīranti yathā manussā
                 gattesu tesaṃ valiyo na honti
                 pucchāmi taṃ yakkha mahānubhāvaṃ 4-
                 kathannu devāna 5- sarīradeho.
          [1534] < PTS. J IV , 109 >Devā na jīranti yathā manussā
                 gattesu tesaṃ valiyo na honti
                 suve suve bhiyyatarova tesaṃ

  Footnote:1 Sī. Ma. chandaṃ. 2 Sī. Yu. āyuṃ ca vaṇṇaṃ ca. 3 Ma. hāyateva tava....
 4 Ma. mahānubhāva. 5 Yu. kathaṃ na devānaṃ.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 312]
                 Dibbo ca vaṇṇo vipulā ca bhogā.
          [1535] < PTS. J IV , 110 >Kiṃsūdha bhītā janatā anekā
                 maggo ca nekāyatanappavutto
                 pucchāmi taṃ yakkha mahānubhāva
                 kattha ṭhito paralokaṃ na bhāye.
          [1536] < PTS. J IV , 110 >Vācaṃ manañca paṇidhāya sammā
                 kāyena pāpāni akubbamāno
                 bahunnapānaṃ gharamāvasanto
                 saddho mudū saṃvibhāgī vadaññū
                 saṅgāhako sakhilo saṇhavāco
                 ettha ṭhito paralokaṃ na bhāye.
     [1537] < PTS. J IV , 111 >Anusāsasi maṃ yakkha yathā mātā yathā pitā
            uḷāravaṇṇa pucchāmi ko nu tvamasi subrahā.
     [1538] < PTS. J IV , 111 >Udayohamasmi kalyāṇi saṅkaratthāyidhāgato 1-
            āmanta kho taṃ gacchāmi muttosmi tava saṅkarā.
     [1539] < PTS. J IV , 111 >Sace kho tvaṃ udayosi saṅkaratthāyidhāgato 1-
            anusāsa maṃ rājaputta yathāssa puna saṅgamo.
          [1540] < PTS. J IV , 111 >Atipatati 2- vayo khaṇo tatheva
                 ṭhānaṃ natthi dhuvaṃ cavanti sattā
                 parijīyati adhuvaṃ sarīraṃ

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote:1 Ma. saṅkarattā idhāgato. Sī. Yu. saṅkaratthā idhāgato. 2 Sī. Yu. adhipatatī.

[page 313]
                 Udaye mā pamāda carassu dhammaṃ.
                 Kasiṇā paṭhavī dhanassa pūrā
                 ekasseva siyā anaññadheyyā
                 taṃ vāpi jahāti 1- avītarāgo
                 udaye mā pamāda carassu dhammaṃ.
                 Mātā pitā ca bhātaro ca bhaginī
                 bhariyā sāmiko sadhanena hoti 2-
                 te vāpi jahanti aññamaññaṃ
                 udaye mā pamāda carassu dhammaṃ.
                 Kāyo parabhojananti viditvā 3-
                 saṃsāre sugatī ca duggatī ca 4-
                 ittaravāsoti jānitvā 5-
                 udaye mā pamāda carassu dhammaṃ.
     [1541] < PTS. J IV , 113 >Sādhu bhāsati 6- yaṃ yakkho appaṃ maccāna jīvitaṃ
            kasirañca parittañca tañca dukkhena saṃyutaṃ
            sāhaṃ ekā pabbajissāmi hitvā kāsiṃ surundhananti.
                      Udayajātakaṃ catutthaṃ.

                        7 Dasarathajātakaṃ
     [1564] < PTS. J IV , 126 >Etha lakkhaṇasītā ca ubho otarathodakaṃ
            evāyaṃ bharato āha rājā dasaratho mato.
     [1565] < PTS. J IV , 126 >Kena rāma pabhāvena socitabbaṃ na socasi
            pitaraṃ kālakataṃ sutvā na taṃ pasahate dukhaṃ.
     [1566] < PTS. J IV , 126 >Yaṃ na sakkā naṃ pāletuṃ 1- posena lapataṃ bahuṃ
            sa kissa viññū medhāvī attānamupatāpaye.
     [1567] < PTS. J IV , 127 >Daharā ca hi ye vuḍḍhā ye bālā ye ca paṇḍitā
            addhā ceva daliddā ca sabbe maccuparāyanā.
     [1568] < PTS. J IV , 127 >Phalānamiva pakkānaṃ niccaṃ patanato bhayaṃ
            evaṃ jātāna maccānaṃ niccaṃ maraṇato bhayaṃ.
     [1569] < PTS. J IV , 127 >Sāyameke na dissanti pāto diṭṭhā bahū janā
            pāto eke na dissanti sāyaṃ diṭṭhā bahū janā.
     [1570] < PTS. J IV , 127 >Paridevayamāno ce kiñcidatthaṃ udabbahe
            sammuḷho hiṃsamattānaṃ kayirā taṃ vicakkhaṇo.
     [1571] < PTS. J IV , 127 >Kīso vivaṇṇo bhavati hiṃsamattānamattano
            na tena petā pālenti niratthā paridevanā.
     [1572] < PTS. J IV , 127 >Yathā saraṇamādittaṃ vārināva nibbāpaye 2-
            evampi dhīro sutavā medhāvī paṇḍito naro
            khippamuppatitaṃ sokaṃ vāto tūlaṃva dhaṃsaye.

                         Tassuddānaṃ
                 siri mātusuposakanāgavaro
                 puna juṇhaka dhammamudayavaro
                 atha pānīya yudhañjayako ca
                 dasaratha saṃvara pāragatena nava.
                     Ekādasanipātaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ.

                       Dvādasanipātajātakaṃ
                       1 cullakuṇālajātakaṃ
     [1601] Khuddānaṃ 1- lahucittānaṃ akataññūna dubbhinaṃ
            nādevasatto puriso thīnaṃ saddhātumarahati.
          [1602] Na tā pajānanti kataṃ na kiccaṃ
                 na mātaraṃ pitaraṃ bhātaraṃ vā
                 anariyā samatikkantadhammā
                 sasseva cittassa vasaṃ vajanti.
          [1603] Cirānuvuṭṭhampi piyaṃ manāpaṃ
                 anukampakaṃ pāṇasamampi santaṃ 2-
                 āvāsu kiccesu ca naṃ jahanti
                 tasmāhaṃ itthīnaṃ na vissasāmi.
          [1604] Thīnañhi cittaṃ yathā vānarassa
                 kannappakannaṃ yathā rukkhachāyā
                 calācalaṃ hadayaṃ itthiyānaṃ
                 cakkassa nemi viya parivattati.
          [1605] Yadā tā passanti samekkhamānā
                 ādeyyarūpaṃ purisassa vittaṃ
                 saṇhāhi vācāhi nayantimenaṃ

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote:1 Ma. luddhānaṃ. 2 Ma. bhattuṃ.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 323]
                 Kambojakā jalajeneva assaṃ.
          [1606] Yadā na passanti samekkhamānā
                 ādeyyarūpaṃ purisassa vittaṃ
                 samantato naṃ parivajjayanti
                 tiṇṇo nadīpāragatova kullaṃ.
          [1607] Silesūpamā sikhīriva sabbabhakkhā
                 tikkhamāyā nadīriva sīghasotā
                 sevanti hetā piyamappiyañca
                 nāvā yathā orakūlaṃ parañca.
     [1608] Na tā ekassa na dvinnaṃ āpaṇova pasārito
            yo tā mayhanti maññeyya vātaṃ jālena bandhaye 1-.
     [1609] Yathā nadī ca pantho ca pānāgāraṃ sabhā papā
            evaṃ lokitthiyo nāma velā tāsaṃ na vijjati.
     [1610] Ghatāsanasamā etā kaṇhasappasirūpamā
            gāvo bahi tiṇasseva omasanti varaṃ varaṃ.
          [1611] Ghatāsanaṃ kuñjaraṃ kaṇhasappaṃ
                 muddhābhisittaṃ pamudā 2- ca sabbā
                 ete naro niccayato 3- bhajetha
                 tesaṃ have dubbidu saccabhāvo.

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote:1 ma bādhaye. 2 Ma. pamadā. 3. Sī. Yu. niccayatto.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 324]
          [1612] Naccantavaṇṇā na bahūna kantā
                 na dakkhiṇā pamudā sevitabbā
                 na parassa bhariyā na dhanassa hetu
                 etitthiyo pañca na sevitabbāti.
                       Cullakuṇālajātakaṃ paṭhamaṃ.
                          ----------
                        2 Bhaddasālajātakaṃ
     [1613] < PTS. J IV , 154 >Kā tvaṃ suddhehi vatthehi aghe vehāyasaṇṭhitā 1-
            kena tyassūni vattanti kuto taṃ bhayamāgataṃ.
     [1614] < PTS. J IV , 154 >Taveva deva vijite bhaddasāloti maṃ vidū
            saṭṭhivassasahassāni tiṭṭhato pūjitassa me.
     [1615] < PTS. J IV , 154 >Kārayantā nagarāni agāre ca disampati
            vividhe cāpi pāsāde na mante accamaññisuṃ
            yatheva mante pūjesuṃ tatheva tvaṃpi pūjaya.
     [1616] < PTS. J IV , 155 >Tañca ahaṃ 2- na passāmi thūlaṃ kāyena te dumaṃ
            ārohaparināhena abhirūposi jātiyā.
     [1617] < PTS. J IV , 155 >Pāsādaṃ kārayissāmi ekathambhaṃ manoramaṃ
            tattha taṃ upanessāmi cirante yakkha jīvitaṃ.
     [1618] < PTS. J IV , 155 >Evaṃ cittaṃ udapādi sarīrena vinābhāvo
            puthuso maṃ vikantetvā khaṇḍaso avakantatha.

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote:1 Sī. Yu. vehāsayaṃ. 2 Ma. taṃ ivāhaṃ.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 325]
     [1619] < PTS. J IV , 156 >Agge ca chetvā majjhe ca pacchā mūlamhi chindatha 1-
            evaṃ me chijjamānassa na dukkhaṃ maraṇaṃ siyā.
     [1620] < PTS. J IV , 156 >Hatthapādaṃ yathā chinde kaṇṇanāsañca jīvato
            tato pacchā siro chinde taṃ dukkhaṃ maraṇaṃ siyā.
     [1621] < PTS. J IV , 156 >Sukhaṃ nu khaṇḍaso chindaṃ 2- bhaddasāla vanappati
            kiṃ hetu kiṃ upādāya khaṇḍaso chindamicchasi.
     [1622] < PTS. J IV , 156 >Yañca hetuṃ upādāya hetudhammūpasañhitaṃ

            tepihaṃ upahiṃseyyaṃ paresaṃ asukhocitaṃ.

            rajjaṃ kārehi bhaddante kiṃ araññe karissasi.
     [1709] < PTS. J IV , 195 >Yathā gilitvā balisaṃ uddhareyya salohitaṃ
            uddharitvā sukhī assa evaṃ passāmi attanā 5-.
     [1710] < PTS. J IV , 195 >Kiṃ nu tvaṃ balisaṃ brūsi kiṃ tvaṃ brūsi salohitaṃ
            kiṃ nu tvaṃ ubbhataṃ brūsi taṃ me akkhāhi pucchito.
     [1711] < PTS. J IV , 195 >Kāmāhaṃ balisaṃ brūmi hatthiassaṃ salohitaṃ
            cattāhaṃ ubbhataṃ brūmi evaṃ jānāhi khattiya.

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote:1 Ma. mahante ṭhapetuṃ. 2 Sī. Yu. sabbo ca. 3 Sī. Yu. ekanto.
 4 Ma. gacchatha. 5 Ma. attānaṃ. Yu. attani.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 340]
     [1712] < PTS. J IV , 196 >Ciñcamāṇavikā mātā devadatto ca me pitā
            ānando paṇḍito nāgo sārīputto ca devatā
            ahaṃ tadā rājaputto 1- evaṃ dhāretha jātakanti.
                      Mahāpadumajātakaṃ navamaṃ.

                      10 Mittāmittajātakaṃ
     [1713] < PTS. J IV , 197 >Kāni kammāni kubbānaṃ kathaṃ viññū parakkame
            amittaṃ jāneyya medhāvī disvā sutvā ca paṇḍito.
     [1714] < PTS. J IV , 197 >Na naṃ umhayate disvā na ca naṃ paṭinandati
            cakkhūnissa 2- na dadāti paṭilomañca vattati.
     [1715] < PTS. J IV , 197 >Amitte tassa bhajati mitte tassa na sevati
            vaṇṇakāme nivāreti akkosante pasaṃsati.
     [1716] < PTS. J IV , 197 >Guyhañca tassa nakkhāti tassa guyhaṃ na gūhati
            kammaṃ tassa na vaṇṇeti paññassa nappasaṃsati.
     [1717] < PTS. J IV , 197 >Abhave nandati tassa bhave tassa na nandati
            acchariyaṃ 3- bhojanaṃ laddhā tassa nuppajjate sati
            tato naṃ nānukampati ahāsopi 4- labheyyito.
     [1718] < PTS. J IV , 197 >Iccete soḷasākārā amittasmiṃ patiṭṭhitā
            yehi amittaṃ jāneyya disvā sutvā ca paṇḍito.
     [1719] < PTS. J IV , 198 >Kāni kammāni kubbānaṃ kathaṃ viññū parakkame

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote:1 Ma. rājaptto ahaṃ āsiṃ. 2 Ma. cakkhūni cassa. 3 Ma. accheraṃ.
 4 Ma. aho sopi.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 341]
            Mittaṃ jāneyya medhāvī disvā sutvā ca paṇḍito.
     [1720] < PTS. J IV , 198 >Pavutthañcassa 1- sarati āgataṃ abhinandati
            tato kelāyiko hoti vācāya paṭinandati.
     [1721] < PTS. J IV , 198 >Mitte tasseva bhajati amitte tassa na sevati
            akkosante nivāreti vaṇṇakāme pasaṃsati.
     [1722] < PTS. J IV , 198 >Guyhañca tassa akkhāti tassa guyhañca gūhati
            kammañca tassa vaṇṇeti paññamassa 2- pasaṃsati.
     [1723] < PTS. J IV , 198 >Bhave ca nandati tassa abhave tassa na nandati
            acchariyaṃ bhojanaṃ laddhā tassa uppajjate sati
            tato naṃ anukampati pahāsopi 3- labheyyito.
     [1724] < PTS. J IV , 198 >Iccete soḷasākārā mittasmiṃ supatiṭṭhitā
            yehi mittañca jāneyya 4- disvā sutvā ca paṇḍitoti.
                       Mittāmittajātakaṃ dasamaṃ.

                            Tassuddānaṃ
                 lahucitta sasāla kasanti puna
                 atha kāma dasakhaluṭṭhānavaro
                 atha kaṇha sukosiya meṇḍavaro
                 padumo puna mittavarena dasa.
                        Dvādasanipātaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ.

                        1 ambajātakaṃ
          [1725] < PTS. J IV , 203 >Ahāsi me ambaphalāni pubbe
                 aṇūni thūlāni ca brahmacārī
                 teheva mantehi nadāni tuyhaṃ
                 dumapphalā pātubhavanti brahme.
          [1726] < PTS. J IV , 203 >Nakkhattayogaṃ paṭimānayāmi
                 khaṇaṃ muhuttañca mantena passaṃ 1-
                 nakkhattayogañca khaṇañca laddhā
                 addhā harissambaphalaṃ bahukaṃ 2-.
          [1727] < PTS. J IV , 204 >Nakkhattayogaṃ na pure abhāṇi
                 khaṇaṃ muhuttaṃ na pure asaṃsi
                 athāhari ambaphalaṃ pahutaṃ 3-
                 vaṇṇena gandhena rasenupetaṃ.
          [1728] < PTS. J IV , 204 >Mantābhijappena purepi 4- tuyhaṃ
                 dumapphalā pātubhavanti brahme
                 svājja na vādesi 5- jappampi mantaṃ 6-
                 ayaṃ so ko nāma tavajja dhammo.

 5 Sī. Ma. pāresi. 6 Sī. Yu. japampi mante.

            madhuṃ madhutthiko vinde so hi tassa dumuttamo.
     [1732] < PTS. J IV , 205 >Khattiyā brāhmaṇā vessā suddā caṇḍālapukkusā

          [1734] < PTS. J IV , 206 >Yathā samaṃ maññamāno pateyya

                       Ambajātakaṃ paṭhamaṃ.

                       2 Phandanajātakaṃ
     [1738] < PTS. J IV , 208 >Kuṭhārihattho puriso vanamoggayha tiṭṭhasi

     [1741] < PTS. J IV , 209 >Kīdisānissa pattāni khandho vā pana kīdiso

     [1746] < PTS. J IV , 210 >Iti phandanarukkhopi veraṃ kappesi devatā 6-
            jātānañca ajātānaṃ īsānaṃ dukkhamāvahi.

                      4 Cullanāradakassapajātakaṃ 3-
     [1764] < PTS. J IV , 221 >Na te kaṭṭhāni bhinnāni na te udakamābhataṃ
            aggipi te na hāsito 4- kinnu mandova jhāyasi.

     [1770] < PTS. J IV , 222 >Āsavo tāta lokasmiṃ surā nāma pavuccati
            manuññā 7- surabhī vaggū madhukhuddarasūpamā 8-

                          5 Dūtajātakaṃ
     [1777] < PTS. J IV , 225 >Dūte te brahme pāhesiṃ gaṅgātīrasmi jhāyato

                 medhāvinaṃ ekamanaṃ viditvā
                 akkheyya tippāni 8- parassa dhīro
                 saṇhaṃ giraṃ atthavatiṃ pamuñce.

 bhavantamittā. 8 Ma. tibbāni.

[page 351]
          [1784] < PTS. J IV , 226 >Sace ca jaññā avisayhamattano

                 saccaṃ hirottappamapekkhamāno.

                      6 Kāliṅgabodhijātakaṃ
     [1790] < PTS. J IV , 232 >Rājā kāliṅgo cakkavatti dhammena paṭhavimanusāsi 1-
            agamāsi 2- bodhisamīpaṃ nāgena mahānubhāvena.
     [1791] < PTS. J IV , 233 >Kāliṅgo bhāradvājo ca rājānaṃ kāliṅgaṃ samaṇakolaññaṃ
            cakkavattayato pariggahetvā 3- pañjalī idamavoca.
     [1792] < PTS. J IV , 233 >Paccoroha mahārāja bhūmibhāgo yathā samaṇugīto 4-
            idha anadhivarā buddhā abhisambuddhā virocanti.
     [1793] < PTS. J IV , 233 >Padakkhiṇato āvaṭṭā tiṇalatā asmiṃ bhūmibhāgasmiṃ
            paṭhaviyā ayaṃ 5- maṇḍo iti no sutaṃ [6]- mahārāja.
     [1794] < PTS. J IV , 233 >Sāgarapariyantāya (medaniyā) sabbabhūtadharaṇiyā
            paṭhaviyā ayaṃ maṇḍo orohitvā namo karohi.

                 yaṃ vāsavaṃ bhūtapatiddasāma
                 sabbeva bhonto sumanā bhavantu
                 yaṃ brāhmaṇo paccupādi bhisāni.
     [1941] < PTS. J IV , 314 >Ahañca sārīputto ca moggallāno ca kassapo
            anuruddho puṇṇānando tadāsuṃ satta bhātaro.
            Bhaginī uppalavaṇṇā [2]- dāsī khujjuttarā tadā
            citto gahapati dāso yakkho sātāgiro tadā.
            Pālileyyo 3- tadā nāgo madhudo 4- seṭṭhavānaro
            kāḷudāyī tadā sakko evaṃ dhāretha jātakanti.
                        Bhisajātakaṃ pañcamaṃ.
                         ----------

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote:1 Sī. paratthambha. Ma. vupatthambha. 2 Ma. ca. 3 Sī. Yu. pārileyyo.
 4 Sī. Yu. madhuvo.

[page 382]
                         6 Surucijātakaṃ
     [1942] < PTS. J IV , 319 >Mahesī rucino 1- bhariyā ānītā paṭhamaṃ ahaṃ
            dasa vassasahassāni yaṃ maṃ suruci ānayi 2-.
            Sāhaṃ brāhmaṇa rājānaṃ vedehaṃ mithilaggahaṃ
            nābhijānāmi kāyena vācāya uda cetasā
            suruciṃ atimaññittha 3- āvi vā yadivā raho.
            Etena saccavajjena putto uppajjataṃ ise
            musā me bhaṇamānāya muddhā phalatu sattadhā.
     [1943] < PTS. J IV , 320 >Bhattu manāpā sassu piyā mātā pitā ca sassuro 4-
            te maṃ brahme vinetāro yāva aṭṭhaṃsu jīvitaṃ.
            Sāhaṃ ahiṃsāratinī kāmasā dhammacārinī
            sakkaccaṃ te upaṭṭhāsiṃ rattindivamatanditā.
            Etena saccavajjena putto uppajjataṃ ise
            musā me bhaṇamānāya muddhā phalatu sattadhā.
     [1944] < PTS. J IV , 320 >Soḷasitthīsahassāni sahabhariyāni brāhmaṇa
            tāsu issā vā kodho vā nāhu mayhaṃ kudācanaṃ.
            Hitena tāsaṃ nandāmi na ca me kāci appiyā
            attānaṃvānukampāmi sadā sabbā sapattiyo.
            Etena saccavajjena putto upajjataṃ ise
            musā me bhaṇamānāya muddhā phalatu sattadhā.

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote:1 Ma. surucino. 2 Ma. surucimānayi. 3 Sī. Yu. atimaññittho.
 4 Ma. bhattu mama sassu mātā pitā cāpi ca sassuro.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 383]
     [1945] < PTS. J IV , 320 >Dāse kammakare pose 1- yecaññe anujīvino
            posemi 2- sahadhammena sadā pamuditindriyā.
            Etena saccavajjena putto uppajjataṃ ise
            musā me bhaṇamānāya muddhā phalatu sattadhā.
     [1946] < PTS. J IV , 320 >Samaṇe brāhmaṇe cāpi aññe cāpi vaṇibbake
            tappemi annapānena sadā payatapāṇinī.
            Etena saccavajjena putto uppajjataṃ ise
            musā me bhaṇamānāya muddhā phalatu sattadhā.
     [1947] < PTS. J IV , 320 >Cātuddasiṃ pañcadasiṃ yā ca pakkhassa aṭṭhamī 3-
            pāṭihāriyapakkhañca aṭṭhaṅgasusamāhitaṃ 4-
            uposathaṃ upavasāmi sadā sīlesu saṃvutā.
            Etena saccavajjena putto uppajjataṃ ise
            musā me bhaṇamānāya muddhā phalatu sattadhā.
     [1948] < PTS. J IV , 321 >Sabbeva te dhammaguṇā rājaputti yasassini
            saṃvijjanti tayi bhadde ye tvaṃ kittesi attani.
            Khattiyo jātisampanno abhijāto yasassimā
            dhammarājā vedehānaṃ 5- putto upajjate tava.
     [1949] < PTS. J IV , 322 >Dummi 6- rajojalladharo aghe vehāyasaṇṭhito
            manuññaṃ bhāsase vācaṃ yaṃ mayhaṃ hadayaṅgamaṃ.

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote:1 Ma. pesse. 2 Ma. pesemi. 3 Sī. Yu. aṭṭhamiṃ. 4 Ma. aṭṭhaṅgasusamāgataṃ.
 5 Ma. videhānaṃ. 6 rumhītipi. Sī. Yu. rummī. Ma. dummī.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 384]
            Devatā nusi saggamhā isi vāpi 1- mahiddhiko
            ko vāsi tvaṃ anuppatto attānaṃ me pavedaya.
     [1950] < PTS. J IV , 322 >Yaṃ devasaṅghā vandanti sudhammāya samāgatā
            sohaṃ sakko sahassakkho āgatosmi tavantike.
            Itthiyo jīvalokasmiṃ yā honti 2- samacārinī
            medhāvinī sīlavatī sassudevā patibbatā.
            Tādisāya sumedhāya sucikammāya nāriyā
            devā dassanamāyanti mānusiyā amānusā.
            Tvañca bhadde suciṇṇena pubbe sucaritena ca
            idha rājakule jātā sabbakāmasamiddhinī.
            Ayañca te rājaputti ubhayattha kaṭaggaho
            devalokūpapatti ca kitti ca idha jīvite.
            Ciraṃ sumedhe sukhinī dhammamattani pālaya
            esāhaṃ tidivaṃ yāmi piyaṃ me tava dassananti.
                       Surucijātakaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ.

                      7 Pañcuposathikajātakaṃ
          [1951] < PTS. J IV , 329 >Appossukkodāni tuvaṃ kapota
                 vihaṅgama na tava bhojanattho
                 khuddaṃ pipāsaṃ adhivāsayanto

                 Kasmā bhavaṃposathiko kapota 1-.
          [1952] < PTS. J IV , 329 >Ahaṃ pure giddhigato kapotiyā

                 athaggahī sākuṇiko kapotiṃ
                 akāmako tāya vinā ahosiṃ.
                 Nānābhāvā 3- vippayogena tassā
                 manomayaṃ vedana vedayāmi 4-
                 tasmā ahaṃposatha 5- pālayāmi
                 rāgo mamaṃ mā punarāgamāsi.

          [1954] < PTS. J IV , 330 >Usabho ahu balavā gāmikassa

                 dukkhābhitunno maraṇaṃ upāgā 7-.
                 Tato janā nikkhamitvāna gāmā
                 kanditvā roditvā apakkamiṃsu
                 tasmā ahaṃposatha pālayāmi

 7 Sī. Yu. upāgami.

[page 386]
                 Kodho mamaṃ mā punarāgamāsi.
          [1955] < PTS. J IV , 330 >Matāna maṃsāni bahū susāne

                 kasmā bhavaṃposathiko sigāla 1-.
          [1956] < PTS. J IV , 330 >Pavissa 2- kucchiṃ mahato gajassa
                 kuṇape rato hatthimaṃsesu giddho 3-
                 uṇho ca vāto tikhiṇā ca rasmiyo
                 te sosayuṃ tassa karīsamaggaṃ.
                 Kīso ca paṇḍū ca ahaṃ bhadante
                 na me ahu nikkhamanāya maggo
                 mahā ca megho sahasā pavassi
                 so temayi tassa karīsamaggaṃ.
                 Tato ahaṃ nikkhamisaṃ bhadante
                 cando yathā rāhumukhā pamutto
                 tasmā ahaṃposatha pālayāmi
                 lobho mamaṃ mā punarāgamāsi.
          [1957] < PTS. J IV , 331 >Vammikathūpasmiṃ kipillikāni

                 Kasmā bhavaṃposathiko nu accha 1-.

                      8 Mahāmorajātakaṃ
          [1961] < PTS. J IV , 337 >Sace hi tyāhaṃ dhanahetu gahito 1-

                 atha kissa maṃ pāsavasūpanītaṃ
                 pamuttave icchasi bandhanasmā.
                 Pāṇātipātā virato nusajja
                 abhayannu te sabbabhūtesu dinnaṃ

          [1967] < PTS. J IV , 338 >Cando ca suriyo ca ubho sudassanā

                 imassa lokassa parassa vā te

          [1968] < PTS. J IV , 339 >Cando ca suriyo ca ubho sudassanā

                 Te tattha kālenupasaṅkamitvā

[page 391]
                 Pucchāhi yaṃ te manaso piyaṃ siyā
                 te taṃ 1- pavakkhanti yathāpajānaṃ

                       9 Tacchasūkarajātakaṃ
     [1975] < PTS. J IV , 344 >Yadesamānā vicarimhā pabbatāni vanāni ca

  Footnote:1 Ma. te te. 2 Ma. tesampahaṃ. 3 Sī. patto. Ma. pattā. 4 Ma.

            Anvesaṃ vipule 1- ñātī teme adhigatā mayā.

            rammā cimā girinajjo 2- phāsuvāso bhavissati.

                     10 Mahāvāṇijajātakaṃ
     [1990] < PTS. J IV , 351 >Vāṇijā samitiṃ katvā nānāraṭṭhato āgatā
            dhanahārā pakkamiṃsu ekaṃ katvāna gāmaṇiṃ.
            Te taṃ kantāramāgamma appabhakkhaṃ anūdakaṃ 4-
            mahānigrodhamaddakkhuṃ sītacchāyaṃ manoramaṃ.
            Te ca tattha nisīditvā tassa rukkhassa chādiyā
            vāṇijā samacintesuṃ bālā mohena pārutā.
            Allāyate 5- ayaṃ rukkho api vārīva 6- sandati
            iṅghassa purimaṃ sākhaṃ mayaṃ chindāma vāṇijā.
            Sā ca chinnāva pagghari acchaṃ vāri 7- anāvilaṃ

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote:1 Ma. pamuditā. 2 Ma. mahānādaṃ panādisuṃ. 3 Ma. te su udumbaramūlasmiṃ.
 4 Ma. anodakaṃ. 5 Sī. Yu. addāyate. 6 Sī. Yu. vārī ca. 7 Ma. vārī.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 395]
            Te tattha nhātvā pivitvā yāvaticchiṃsu vāṇijā.
            Dutiyaṃ samacintesuṃ bālā mohena pārutā
            iṅghassa dakkhiṇaṃ sākhaṃ mayaṃ chindāma vāṇijā.
            Sā ca chinnāva pagghari sālimaṃsodanaṃ bahuṃ
            appodavaṇṇe kummāse siṅgividalasūpiyo
            te tattha bhutvā khāditvā yāvaticchiṃsu vāṇijā.
            Tatiyaṃ samacintesuṃ bālā mohena pārutā
            iṅghassa pacchimaṃ sākhaṃ mayaṃ chindāma vāṇijā.
            Sā ca chinnāva pagghari nāriyo samalaṅkatā
            vicittavatthābharaṇā āmuttamaṇikuṇḍalā.
            Apisu vāṇijā ekā nāriyo pañcavīsati
            samantā parikariṃsu 1- tassa rukkhassa chādiyā
            te tāhi parivāretvā 2- yāvaticchiṃsu vāṇijā.
            Catutthaṃ samacintesuṃ bālā mohena pārutā
            iṅghassa uttaraṃ sākhaṃ mayaṃ chindāma vāṇijā.
            Sā ca chinnāva pagghari muttā veḷuriyā bahū
            rajataṃ jātarūpañca kuttiyo paṭiyāni ca
            kāsikāni ca vatthāni uddhiyāni 3- ca kambalā
            te tattha bhāre bandhitvā yāvaticchiṃsu vāṇijā.
            Pañcamaṃ samacintesuṃ bālā mohena pārutā

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote:1 Ma. parivāriṃsu. 2 Ma. paricāretvā. 3 uddiyāni. Sī. Yu. uddiyāne ca
 kammabale.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 396]
            Iṅghassa mūle 1- chindāma api bhiyyo labhāmase.
            Athuṭṭhahi satthavāho yācamāno katañjali
            nigrodho kiṃ aparajjhati 2- vāṇijā bhaddamatthu te.
            Vāridā purimā sākhā annapānañca dakkhiṇā
            nāridā pacchimā sākhā sabbakāme ca uttarā
            nigrodho kiṃ aparajjhati 2- vāṇijā bhaddamatthu te.
            Yassa rukkhassa chāyāya nisīdeyya sayeyya vā
            na tassa sākhaṃ bhañjeyya mittadubbho hi pāpako.
            Te ca tassa anāditvā 3- ekassa vacanaṃ bahū
            nisitāhi kuṭhārīhi mūlato naṃ upakkamuṃ.
     [1991] < PTS. J IV , 353 >Tato nāgā nikkhamiṃsu sannaddhā pañcavīsati
            dhanuggahānaṃ tisatā chasahassā ca cammino 4-.
     [1992] < PTS. J IV , 354 >Ete hanatha bandhatha mā vo muñcittha jīvitaṃ
            ṭhapetvā satthavāhaṃva sabbe bhasmaṃ karotha ne.
     [1993] < PTS. J IV , 354 >Tasmāhi paṇḍito poso sampassaṃ atthamattano
            lobhassa na vasaṃ gacche haneyya disakaṃ manaṃ 5-
            evamādīnavaṃ 6- ñatvā taṇhā dukkhassa sambhavaṃ
            vītataṇho anādāno sato bhikkhu paribbajeti.
                      Mahāvāṇijajātakaṃ dasamaṃ.
